# Define the list of FAANG ticker symbols for clarity and reuse.
faang_tickers = ('AMZN', 'AAPL', 'GOOGL', 'META', 'NFLX')

# Filter customers who have a '.com' email and have made at least one transaction
# with a FAANG stock.
filtered_customers = Customers.WHERE(
    (ENDSWITH(email, '.com')) &
    (HAS(transactions_made.WHERE(ISIN(ticker.symbol, faang_tickers))) == 1)
)

# Calculate the total count of these distinct customers.
result = Broker.CALCULATE(
    num_customers = COUNT(filtered_customers)
)

INFO:httpx:HTTP Request: POST https://us-east5-aiplatform.googleapis.com/v1/projects/solid-drive-448717-p8/locations/us-east5/publishers/anthropic/models/claude-opus-4@20250514:streamRawPredict "HTTP/1.1 200 OK"
[DEBUG] [Q4] Code generated by claude:
# Get transactions from the last 8 weeks excluding current week
# Current week starts at the most recent start of week, so we need to go back 1 week first
# then get 8 weeks worth of data
transactions_info = Transactions.WHERE(
    # Transaction date is between 9 weeks ago and 1 week ago
    (date_time >= DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-9 weeks')) &
    (date_time < DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-1 week'))
).CALCULATE(
    # Calculate week start date for grouping
    week_start = DATETIME(date_time, 'start of week'),
    # Check if transaction occurred on weekend (Saturday=7, Sunday=1 based on start_of_week config)
    is_weekend = (DAYNAME(date_time) == "Saturday") | (DAYNAME(date_time) == "Sunday")
)

# Group by week and calculate counts
result = transactions_info.PARTITION(name="weeks", by=week_start).CALCULATE(
    week_start = week_start,
    total_transactions = COUNT(Transactions),
    weekend_transactions = SUM(Transactions.is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week_start.ASC())
[DEBUG] [Q4] DataFrame from claude is valid

[DEBUG] [Q4] Code generated by gemini:
# Define the time window for the last 8 weeks, excluding the current week
time_window = Broker.CALCULATE(
    start_date=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-8 weeks'),
    end_date=DATETIME('now', 'start of week')
)

# Filter transactions to include only stocks within the defined time window
# and add a 'week_start' field for grouping
transactions_to_group = time_window.Transactions.WHERE(
    (ticker.ticker_type == 'stock') &
    (date_time >= start_date) &
    (date_time < end_date)
).CALCULATE(
    week_start=DATETIME(date_time, 'start of week')
)

# Partition the filtered transactions by week and calculate the required counts
result = transactions_to_group.PARTITION(name="weekly_groups", by=week_start).CALCULATE(
    week_start_date=week_start,
    total_transactions=COUNT(Transactions),
    weekend_transactions=COUNT(Transactions.WHERE(ISIN(DAYNAME(date_time), ("Saturday", "Sunday"))))
).ORDER_BY(week_start_date.ASC())

# and that occurred last week (from the previous Sunday to Saturday).
# It then calculates the total count and sum of the transaction amounts for the filtered set.

    (date_time >= DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-7 days')) &
    (date_time < DATETIME('now', 'start of week'))
)

buy_transactions_past_10_days = Transactions.WHERE(

[DEBUG] [Q8] Code generated by claude:
# Get customers who joined in the last 6 months excluding current month
# First calculate the date range
recent_customers = Customers.WHERE(
    (DATEDIFF("months", join_date, DATETIME("now")) <= 6) & 
    (DATEDIFF("months", join_date, DATETIME("now")) > 0)
).CALCULATE(
    join_month = JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(join_date), LPAD(MONTH(join_date), 2, "0"))
)

# Partition by join month to calculate metrics
monthly_metrics = recent_customers.PARTITION(
    name="months", 
    by=join_month
).CALCULATE(
    month = join_month,
    pmcs = COUNT(Customers),
    pmat = AVG(Customers.transactions_made.amount)
)

result = monthly_metrics.ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# Define the start and end dates for the 6-month period excluding the current month.
start_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-6 months')
end_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')

# Filter customers who joined within the specified date range.
recent_customers = Customers.WHERE((join_date >= start_date) & (join_date < end_date))

# Add a 'signup_month' column in 'YYYY-MM' format to be used for grouping.
customers_by_month = recent_customers.CALCULATE(
    signup_month=JOIN_STRINGS('-', YEAR(join_date), LPAD(MONTH(join_date), 2, '0'))
)

# Partition the filtered customers by their signup month and calculate the required metrics.
result = customers_by_month.PARTITION(name="monthly_signups", by=(signup_month)).CALCULATE(
    month=signup_month,
    PMCS=COUNT(Customers),
    PMAT=AVG(Customers.transactions_made.amount)
)

[DEBUG] [Q8] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Define the date range for the last 6 months, excluding the current month.
date_info = Broker.CALCULATE(
    start_date=DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-6 months'),
    end_date=DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
)

# Step 2: Filter customers who joined within this date range and create a formatted 'signup_month' column.
filtered_customers = date_info.Customers.WHERE(
    (join_date >= start_date) & (join_date < end_date)
).CALCULATE(
    signup_month=JOIN_STRINGS('-', YEAR(join_date), LPAD(MONTH(join_date), 2, '0'))
)

# Step 3: Partition the filtered customers by their signup month and calculate the required metrics.
# PMCS (Per Month Customer Signups) is the count of customers in each group.
# PMAT (Per Month Average Transaction) is the average transaction amount for customers who signed up in that month.
result = filtered_customers.PARTITION(name="monthly_signups", by=(signup_month)).CALCULATE(
    month=signup_month,
    PMCS=COUNT(Customers),
    PMAT=AVG(Customers.transactions_made.amount)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

    (DATEDIFF("months", join_date, DATETIME("now")) > 0)
).CALCULATE(
    join_month = JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(join_date), LPAD(MONTH(join_date), 2, "0"))
)

# Partition by join month to calculate metrics
monthly_metrics = recent_customers.PARTITION(
    name="months", 
    by=join_month
).CALCULATE(
    month = join_month,
    pmcs = COUNT(Customers),
    pmat = AVG(Customers.transactions_made.amount)
)

result = monthly_metrics.ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# Step 1: Define the date range for the last 6 months, excluding the current month.
date_info = Broker.CALCULATE(
    start_date=DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-6 months'),
    end_date=DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
)

# Step 2: Filter customers who joined within this date range and create a formatted 'signup_month' column.
filtered_customers = date_info.Customers.WHERE(
    (join_date >= start_date) & (join_date < end_date)
).CALCULATE(
    signup_month=JOIN_STRINGS('-', YEAR(join_date), LPAD(MONTH(join_date), 2, '0'))
)

# Step 3: Partition the filtered customers by their signup month and calculate the required metrics.
# PMCS (Per Month Customer Signups) is the count of customers in each group.
# PMAT (Per Month Average Transaction) is the average transaction amount for customers who signed up in that month.
result = filtered_customers.PARTITION(name="monthly_signups", by=(signup_month)).CALCULATE(
    month=signup_month,
    PMCS=COUNT(Customers),
    PMAT=AVG(Customers.transactions_made.amount)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

    HAS(transactions_of.WHERE(
        (transaction_type == "sell") & 
        (DATEDIFF("days", date_time, DATETIME("now")) <= 30)

).CALCULATE(

# Define a filter for sell transactions that occurred in the past month.
sell_transactions_last_month = Tickers.transactions_of.WHERE(
    (transaction_type == 'Sell') & (date_time >= DATETIME('now', '-1 month'))
)

# Calculate SPM for each ticker with relevant transactions.
result = Tickers.WHERE(
    HAS(sell_transactions_last_month) == 1
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=symbol,
    total_amount_from_sells=SUM(sell_transactions_last_month.amount),
    total_costs=SUM(sell_transactions_last_month.tax) + SUM(sell_transactions_last_month.commission)
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    SPM=IFF(
        total_amount_from_sells > 0,
        (total_amount_from_sells - total_costs) / total_amount_from_sells * 100,
        0
    )
)

[DEBUG] [Q12] Code generated by gemini:
result = Tickers.WHERE(
    HAS(transactions_of.WHERE(
        (transaction_type == 'Sell') & (date_time >= DATETIME('now', '-1 month'))
    )) == 1
).CALCULATE(
    total_amount=SUM(transactions_of.WHERE((transaction_type == 'Sell') & (date_time >= DATETIME('now', '-1 month'))).amount),
    total_tax=SUM(transactions_of.WHERE((transaction_type == 'Sell') & (date_time >= DATETIME('now', '-1 month'))).tax),
    total_commission=SUM(transactions_of.WHERE((transaction_type == 'Sell') & (date_time >= DATETIME('now', '-1 month'))).commission),
    ticker_symbol=symbol
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    SPM=IFF(
        total_amount > 0,
        (total_amount - (total_tax + total_commission)) / total_amount * 100.0,
        0.0
    )

# Step 1: Extract month and symbol from daily prices
price_info = DailyPrices.CALCULATE(
    month = JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(date), LPAD(MONTH(date), 2, "0")),
    symbol = ticker.symbol,
    close_price = close,
    high_price = high,
    low_price = low
)

# Step 2: Group by symbol and month to calculate statistics
monthly_stats = price_info.PARTITION(name="months", by=(symbol, month)).CALCULATE(
    symbol = symbol,
    month = month,
    avg_close = AVG(DailyPrices.close),
    max_high = MAX(DailyPrices.high),
    min_low = MIN(DailyPrices.low)
)

# Step 3: Calculate MoMC using PREV window function
result = monthly_stats.CALCULATE(
    symbol = symbol,
    month = month,
    avg_close = avg_close,
    max_high = max_high,
    min_low = min_low,
    momc = (avg_close - PREV(avg_close, by=(symbol.ASC(), month.ASC()), per="symbol")) / PREV(avg_close, by=(symbol.ASC(), month.ASC()), per="symbol")
).ORDER_BY(symbol.ASC(), month.ASC())

price_info = DailyPrices.CALCULATE(
    symbol=ticker.symbol,
    month=JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(date), LPAD(MONTH(date), 2, "0"))
)

# Step 2: Partition by ticker and month to calculate monthly aggregates.
monthly_stats = price_info.PARTITION(name="ticker_months", by=(symbol, month)).CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month,
    avg_close=AVG(DailyPrices.close),
    max_high=MAX(DailyPrices.high),
    min_low=MIN(DailyPrices.low)
)

# Step 3: Repartition by symbol to create a per-ticker context, then use the PREV window function to calculate MoMC.
result = monthly_stats.PARTITION(name="tickers", by=(symbol)).ticker_months.CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month,
    avg_close=avg_close,
    max_high=max_high,
    min_low=min_low,
    prev_month_avg_close=PREV(avg_close, by=month.ASC(), default=None)
).CALCULATE(

    avg_close=avg_close,
    max_high=max_high,
    min_low=min_low,
    momc=IFF(prev_month_avg_close != None, (avg_close - prev_month_avg_close) / prev_month_avg_close, None)
)

# Step 1: Augment DailyPrices with symbol and a 'YYYY-MM' month string.
price_info = DailyPrices.CALCULATE(
    symbol=ticker.symbol,
    month=JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(date), LPAD(MONTH(date), 2, "0")),
    high=high,
    low=low,
    close=close
)

# Step 2: Partition by symbol and month to calculate monthly aggregates.
monthly_stats = price_info.PARTITION(name="months", by=(symbol, month)).CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month,
    avg_close=AVG(price_info.close),
    max_high=MAX(price_info.high),
    min_low=MIN(price_info.low)
)

# Step 3: Partition the monthly stats by symbol to group all months for each ticker.
symbol_groups = monthly_stats.PARTITION(name="symbol_groups", by=(symbol))

# Step 4: For each ticker's group of months, calculate the previous month's average close and then the MoMC.
result = symbol_groups.months.CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month,
    average_closing_price=avg_close,
    highest_price=max_high,
    lowest_price=min_low,
    prev_avg_close=PREV(avg_close, by=month.ASC(), per="symbol_groups")
).CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month,
    average_closing_price=average_closing_price,
    highest_price=highest_price,
    lowest_price=lowest_price,
    MoMC=IFF(
        (prev_avg_close != None) & (prev_avg_close != 0),
        (average_closing_price - prev_avg_close) / prev_avg_close,
        None
    )
)

    join_month=MONTH(join_date),
    num_transactions_in_join_month=COUNT(
        transactions_made.WHERE(
            (YEAR(date_time) == YEAR(join_date)) & 
            (MONTH(date_time) == MONTH(join_date))
        )
    )
)

# Find the customer with the highest number of transactions in their join month
result = customer_signup_transactions.TOP_K(
    1, 
    by=num_transactions_in_join_month.DESC()
).CALCULATE(
    _id=customer_id,
    name=customer_name,
    num_transactions=num_transactions_in_join_month
)

# First, calculate the state and ticker type for each transaction
transaction_info = Transactions.CALCULATE(
    customer_state = customer.state,
    ticker_type = ticker.ticker_type
)

# Partition by the combination of customer state and ticker type
# and count the transactions in each group
result = transaction_info.PARTITION(
    name="state_type_groups", 
    by=(customer_state, ticker_type)
).CALCULATE(
    customer_state = customer_state,
    ticker_type = ticker_type,
    num_transactions = COUNT(Transactions)
).TOP_K(5, by=num_transactions.DESC())

q1_2023_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (status == 'success') & 
    (YEAR(date_time) == 2023) & 
    (MONTH(date_time) >= 1) &

).CALCULATE(

)

# Step 1: Get sales with sale price over $30,000 and their payments
high_value_sales = Sales.WHERE(sale_price > 30000)

# Step 2: Get payments from these sales and calculate week start date and weekend flag
payments_info = high_value_sales.payment.CALCULATE(
    payment_date=payment_date,
    week_start=DATETIME(payment_date, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend=ISIN(DAYOFWEEK(payment_date), (1, 7))  # 1=Sunday, 7=Saturday
)

# Step 3: Filter for last 8 weeks excluding current week
# Current week start
current_week_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')

# Filter payments from the last 8 weeks (excluding current week)
last_8_weeks_payments = payments_info.WHERE(
    (week_start < current_week_start) & 
    (week_start >= DATETIME(current_week_start, '-8 weeks'))
)

# Step 4: Group by week and calculate totals
result = last_8_weeks_payments.PARTITION(name="weeks", by=week_start).CALCULATE(
    week=week_start,
    total_payments_received=COUNT(payment),
    weekend_payments_received=SUM(is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week.ASC())

# First, filter payments to include only those from sales over $30,000 and within the last 8 weeks.
# Then, calculate the week's start date and a flag for weekend payments.
payment_info = PaymentsReceived.WHERE(
    (sale_record.sale_price > 30000) &
    (payment_date >= DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-8 weeks')) &
    (payment_date < DATETIME('now', 'start of week'))
).CALCULATE(
    week = DATETIME(payment_date, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend = ISIN(DAYNAME(payment_date), ("Saturday", "Sunday"))
)

# Partition the filtered payments by week and calculate the total and weekend payment counts.
weekly_payments_summary = payment_info.PARTITION(name="weeks", by=(week)).CALCULATE(
    week = week,
    total_payments_received = COUNT(PaymentsReceived),
    weekend_payments_received = SUM(is_weekend)
)

# Order the final result by week in ascending order.
result = weekly_payments_summary.ORDER_BY(week.ASC())

[DEBUG] [Q32] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Filter sales with a price over $30,000 and get their associated payments.
high_value_payments = Sales.WHERE(sale_price > 30000).payment

# Step 2: Filter these payments to include only those made in the last 8 calendar weeks (excluding the current week).
# Also, calculate the start date of the week for each payment and a flag to identify weekend payments.
payments_info = high_value_payments.WHERE(
    (payment_date >= DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-8 weeks')) &
    (payment_date < DATETIME('now', 'start of week'))
).CALCULATE(
    week_start = DATETIME(payment_date, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend = ISIN(DAYNAME(payment_date), ("Saturday", "Sunday"))
)

# Step 3: Group the filtered payments by the start of the week.
weekly_groups = payments_info.PARTITION(name="weekly_groups", by=(week_start))

# Step 4: For each week, calculate the total number of payments and the count of weekend payments.
# The results are then ordered by week in ascending order.
result = weekly_groups.CALCULATE(
    week = week_start,
    total_payments_received = COUNT(payments_info),
    weekend_payments_received = SUM(payments_info.is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week.ASC())

# First, get cars with their latest inventory status
# For each car, we need to find the inventory snapshot with the most recent date
cars_with_status = Cars.WHERE(
    HAS(sale_records) == 1  # Only cars that have been sold
).CALCULATE(
    car_make=make,
    car_model=model,
    # Get the latest inventory status by finding the snapshot with the highest ranking by date
    latest_inventory_status=inventory_snapshots.WHERE(
        RANKING(by=snapshot_date.DESC(), per="Cars") == 1
    ).SINGULAR().is_in_inventory,
    # Get the maximum sale price for this car
    max_sale_price=MAX(sale_records.sale_price)
)

# Filter for cars that are not in inventory (latest status is False)
sold_not_in_inventory = cars_with_status.WHERE(
    latest_inventory_status == False
)

# Group by make and model to find the highest sale price for each combination
result = sold_not_in_inventory.PARTITION(
    name="make_model_groups", 
    by=(car_make, car_model)
).CALCULATE(
    make=car_make,
    model=car_model,
    highest_sale_price=MAX(Cars.max_sale_price)
).ORDER_BY(highest_sale_price.DESC())

[DEBUG] [Q37] Code generated by gemini:
# Define a reusable expression to get the latest inventory status for a car.
# It ranks the inventory snapshots by date for each car and takes the top one.
latest_inventory_status = Cars.inventory_snapshots.WHERE(
    RANKING(by=snapshot_date.DESC(), per="Cars") == 1
).SINGULAR().is_in_inventory

# The main query to find the highest sale price for each make and model.
highest_sales_by_make_model = Cars.WHERE(
    # Filter for cars that have at least one sale record.
    (HAS(sale_records) == 1) &
    # Filter for cars where the latest inventory status is False (not in inventory).
    (latest_inventory_status == False)
).PARTITION(
    # Group the filtered cars by their make and model.
    name="make_model_groups", by=(make, model)
).CALCULATE(
    # For each group, select the make and model.
    make=make,
    model=model,
    # Calculate the maximum sale price from all sales records within the group.
    highest_sale_price=MAX(Cars.sale_records.sale_price)
).ORDER_BY(
    # Order the final results by the highest sale price in descending order.
    highest_sale_price.DESC()
)

        RANKING(by=snapshot_date.DESC(), per="Cars") == 1
    ).SINGULAR().is_in_inventory,
    # Get the maximum sale price for this car
    max_sale_price=MAX(sale_records.sale_price)
)

# Filter for cars that are not in inventory (latest status is False)
sold_not_in_inventory = cars_with_status.WHERE(
    latest_inventory_status == False
)

# Group by make and model to find the highest sale price for each combination
result = sold_not_in_inventory.PARTITION(
    name="make_model_groups", 
    by=(car_make, car_model)
).CALCULATE(
    make=car_make,
    model=car_model,
    highest_sale_price=MAX(Cars.max_sale_price)
).ORDER_BY(highest_sale_price.DESC())

# First, get all salespersons hired between 2022 and 2023
eligible_salespersons = Salespersons.WHERE(
    (YEAR(hire_date) >= 2022) & (YEAR(hire_date) <= 2023)
)

# Get sales for the last 6 months excluding current month
sales_info = eligible_salespersons.sales_made.CALCULATE(
    salesperson_id = salesperson._id,
    sale_month = DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of month'),
    sale_price = sale_price,
    current_month_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of month'),
    six_months_ago = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-6 months')
).WHERE(
    (sale_date >= six_months_ago) & (sale_date < current_month_start)
)

# Partition by salesperson and month
result = sales_info.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_sales", 
    by=(salesperson_id, sale_month)
).CALCULATE(
    month = sale_month,
    salesperson_id = salesperson_id,
    pmsps = COUNT(sales_made),
    pmsr = SUM(sales_made.sale_price)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# Define the sales made by salespersons hired between 2022 and 2023.
target_sales_by_hire_date = Dealership.Sales.WHERE(
    ISIN(YEAR(salesperson.hire_date), (2022, 2023))
)

# Create a scaffold of all unique months within the last 6 months (excluding the current one).
# This assumes that at least one sale of any kind was made in each of these months
# to ensure the month appears in the scaffold.
month_scaffold = Dealership.Sales.CALCULATE(
    sale_month=DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of month')
).PARTITION(name="months", by=(sale_month)).CALCULATE(
    month=sale_month
).WHERE(
    (sale_month >= DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-6 months')) &
    (sale_month < DATETIME('now', 'start of month'))
)

# For each month in the scaffold, calculate the count (PMSPS) and revenue (PMSR) of sales
# from the target salespersons, defaulting to 0 if no sales occurred.
monthly_sales_metrics = month_scaffold.CALCULATE(
    month=month,
    PMSPS=COUNT(target_sales_by_hire_date.WHERE(DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of month') == month)),
    PMSR=DEFAULT_TO(SUM(target_sales_by_hire_date.WHERE(DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of month') == month).sale_price), 0.0)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# Define date boundaries for the last 6 months, excluding the current month.
start_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-6 months')
end_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')

# Filter sales to the relevant time window and create a month column.
# Then, partition by month to create a scaffold of all active months in the period.
monthly_sales_groups = Sales.CALCULATE(
    sale_month=DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of month')
).WHERE(
    (sale_month >= start_date) & (sale_month < end_date)
).PARTITION(name="monthly_groups", by=sale_month)

# For each month, calculate PMSPS and PMSR for the specified salesperson cohort.
# This approach ensures that if a month had any sales at all (even not from the cohort),
# it will be included in the result, with metrics correctly calculated as 0 if the cohort had no sales.
pmsps_pmsr_by_month = monthly_sales_groups.CALCULATE(
    month=sale_month,
    PMSPS=COUNT(Sales.WHERE(
        (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) >= 2022) & (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) <= 2023)
    )),
    PMSR=DEFAULT_TO(SUM(Sales.WHERE(
        (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) >= 2022) & (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) <= 2023)
    ).sale_price), 0.0)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# Filter salespersons hired between 2022 and 2023 and get their sales in the last 6 months
filtered_sales = Salespersons.WHERE(
    (YEAR(hire_date) >= 2022) & (YEAR(hire_date) <= 2023)
).sales_made.CALCULATE(
    salesperson_id = salesperson._id,
    salesperson_name = JOIN_STRINGS(' ', salesperson.first_name, salesperson.last_name),
    sale_month = DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of month'),
    sale_price = sale_price
).WHERE(
    (DATEDIFF('months', sale_month, DATETIME('now', 'start of month')) > 0) &
    (DATEDIFF('months', sale_month, DATETIME('now', 'start of month')) <= 6)
)

# Partition by salesperson and month to calculate metrics
result = filtered_sales.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_groups", 
    by=(salesperson_id, salesperson_name, sale_month)
).CALCULATE(
    salesperson_id = salesperson_id,
    salesperson_name = salesperson_name,
    month = sale_month,
    pmsps = COUNT(sales_made),
    pmsr = SUM(sales_made.sale_price)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC(), salesperson_name.ASC())

    payment_year = YEAR(payment_date),
    payment_month = MONTH(payment_date),
    payment_amount = payment_amount
)

# Group by year and month to get monthly totals

).CALCULATE(

    month = payment_month,

    year = year,
    month = month,
    total_payments_received = total_payments,

    payment_year=YEAR(payment_date),
    payment_month=MONTH(payment_date)
)

# Step 2: Group payments by year and month, and calculate the total amount for each group.

    name="monthly_groups", by=(payment_year, payment_month)
).CALCULATE(

    total_payments_received=SUM(PaymentsReceived.payment_amount)
)

    month=JOIN_STRINGS('-', payment_year, LPAD(payment_month, 2, '0')),

[DEBUG] [Q44] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Extract year and month from each payment record.
payment_info = PaymentsReceived.CALCULATE(
    payment_year=YEAR(payment_date),
    payment_month=MONTH(payment_date)
)

# Step 2: Group by year and month, and sum the payment amounts for each group.
# This creates a new collection where each row represents a month that has payment data.
monthly_totals = payment_info.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_groups", by=(payment_year, payment_month)
).CALCULATE(
    payment_year=payment_year,
    payment_month=payment_month,
    total_payments=SUM(PaymentsReceived.payment_amount)
)

# Step 3: Calculate the Month-over-Month (MoM) change and format the output.
# The PREV function retrieves the total_payments from the previous month, ordered chronologically.
# For the first month in the dataset, PREV returns the specified default value. By setting
# default=total_payments, the MoM change for the first month correctly results in zero.
result = monthly_totals.CALCULATE(
    month=JOIN_STRINGS('-', payment_year, LPAD(payment_month, 2, '0')),
    total_payments=total_payments,
    mom_change=total_payments - PREV(total_payments, by=(payment_year.ASC(), payment_month.ASC()), default=total_payments)
).ORDER_BY(payment_year.ASC(), payment_month.ASC())

INFO:httpx:HTTP Request: POST https://us-east5-aiplatform.googleapis.com/v1/projects/solid-drive-448717-p8/locations/us-east5/publishers/anthropic/models/claude-opus-4@20250514:streamRawPredict "HTTP/1.1 200 OK"
[DEBUG] [Q57] Code generated by claude:
# Find top 3 sales representatives by revenue in past 3 months
result = Salespersons.WHERE(
    HAS(sales_made.WHERE(
        DATEDIFF("days", sale_date, DATETIME("now")) <= 90
    )) == 1
).CALCULATE(
    first_name=first_name,
    last_name=last_name,
    total_sales=COUNT(sales_made.WHERE(
        DATEDIFF("days", sale_date, DATETIME("now")) <= 90
    )),
    total_revenue=SUM(sales_made.WHERE(
        DATEDIFF("days", sale_date, DATETIME("now")) <= 90
    ).sale_price)
).TOP_K(3, by=total_revenue.DESC())
WARNING:sqlglot:DATEDIFF unsupported for 'DAYS'.

# Identify the top 3 salespersons by revenue over the defined period.
top_sales_reps_by_revenue = Salespersons.CALCULATE(
    first_name=first_name,
    last_name=last_name,
    total_sales=COUNT(sales_in_period),
    total_revenue=SUM(sales_in_period.sale_price)
).TOP_K(3, by=total_revenue.DESC())

[DEBUG] [Q60] Code generated by claude:
# First, let's get the payments from the previous ISO week
# We need to calculate the date range for the previous ISO week
# Start of current week (ISO weeks start on Monday)
current_week_start = Dealership.CALCULATE(
    current_date=DATETIME('now'),
    current_week_start=DATETIME('now', 'start of week')
)

# Filter payments for the previous week and calculate daily totals by payment method
result = current_week_start.PaymentsReceived.WHERE(
    (payment_date >= DATETIME(current_week_start, '-7 days')) & 
    (payment_date < current_week_start)
).PARTITION(
    name="daily_method_groups", 
    by=(payment_date, payment_method)
).CALCULATE(
    payment_date=payment_date,
    payment_method=payment_method,
    daily_total=SUM(PaymentsReceived.payment_amount)
).ORDER_BY(payment_date.ASC(), payment_method.ASC())

[DEBUG] [Q60] Code generated by gemini:
# Define the start and end dates for the previous week
date_boundaries = Dealership.CALCULATE(
    start_of_last_week=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-7 days'),
    end_of_last_week=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-1 day')
)

# Filter payments to include only those from the previous week
payments_in_last_week = date_boundaries.PaymentsReceived.WHERE(
    (payment_date >= start_of_last_week) & (payment_date <= end_of_last_week)
)

# Group the filtered payments by date and payment method, and calculate the total amount for each group
daily_payment_totals = payments_in_last_week.PARTITION(
    name="daily_payments_by_method",
    by=(payment_date, payment_method)
).CALCULATE(
    payment_day=payment_date,
    method=payment_method,
    total_received=SUM(PaymentsReceived.payment_amount)
)

result = daily_payment_totals

INFO:google_genai.models:AFC is enabled with max remote calls: 10.
INFO:httpx:HTTP Request: POST https://us-east5-aiplatform.googleapis.com/v1/projects/solid-drive-448717-p8/locations/us-east5/publishers/anthropic/models/claude-opus-4@20250514:streamRawPredict "HTTP/1.1 200 OK"
[DEBUG] [Q61] Code generated by claude:
# First, filter sales for 2023 and calculate quarter information
sales_2023 = Sales.WHERE(YEAR(sale_date) == 2023).CALCULATE(
    sale_price=sale_price,
    customer_state=customer.state,
    quarter=IFF(MONTH(sale_date) <= 3, 1,
             IFF(MONTH(sale_date) <= 6, 2,
             IFF(MONTH(sale_date) <= 9, 3, 4))),
    quarter_start_date=IFF(MONTH(sale_date) <= 3, "2023-01-01",
                       IFF(MONTH(sale_date) <= 6, "2023-04-01",
                       IFF(MONTH(sale_date) <= 9, "2023-07-01", "2023-10-01")))
)

# Group by state and quarter, then calculate total sales
result = sales_2023.PARTITION(
    name="state_quarter_groups", 
    by=(customer_state, quarter_start_date)
).CALCULATE(
    state=customer_state,
    quarter_start_date=quarter_start_date,
    total_sales=SUM(Sales.sale_price)
).ORDER_BY(state.ASC(), quarter_start_date.ASC())

[DEBUG] [Q61] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Filter sales for 2023 and calculate the customer's state and the start date of the quarter for each sale.
sales_2023_with_quarter = Sales.WHERE(YEAR(sale_date) == 2023).CALCULATE(
    customer_state=customer.state,
    # Calculate the first day of the quarter for the sale_date using nested IFF statements.
    quarter_start_date=IFF(
        ISIN(MONTH(sale_date), (1, 4, 7, 10)),
        DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of month'),
        IFF(
            ISIN(MONTH(sale_date), (2, 5, 8, 11)),
            DATETIME(DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of month'), '-1 month'),
            DATETIME(DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of month'), '-2 months')
        )
    )
)

# Step 2: Partition the data by state and quarter, then calculate the total sales for each group.
result = sales_2023_with_quarter.PARTITION(
    name="state_quarters",
    by=("customer_state", "quarter_start_date")

# Get treatments from the last 3 months excluding current month
treatments_last_3_months = Treatments.CALCULATE(
    treatment_month = JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(start_dt), LPAD(MONTH(start_dt), 2, "0")),
    patient_id = patient_id,
    is_biologic = drug.drug_type == "biologic"
).WHERE(

    DATEDIFF("months", start_dt, DATETIME("now")) <= 3
)

    name="months", 
    by=treatment_month
).CALCULATE(
    month = treatment_month,
    patient_count = NDISTINCT(Treatments.patient_id),
    biologic_treatment_count = NDISTINCT(Treatments.WHERE(is_biologic == 1).patient_id)
).ORDER_BY(month.DESC())

start_of_current_month = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
start_of_3_months_ago = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-3 months')

# Filter treatments to the relevant time period and extract necessary information
treatment_info = Treatments.WHERE(
    (start_dt >= start_of_3_months_ago) & (start_dt < start_of_current_month)
).CALCULATE(
    treatment_month=DATETIME(start_dt, 'start of month'),
    patient_id=patient_id,
    is_biologic=(drug.drug_type == 'biologic')
)

    month=treatment_month,
    patient_count=NDISTINCT(monthly_treatments.patient_id),
    biologic_treatment_count=NDISTINCT(KEEP_IF(monthly_treatments.patient_id, monthly_treatments.is_biologic == True))

# Define the time window for the last 3 full months
start_of_period = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-3 months')
end_of_period = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')

# Filter treatments to the relevant period and extract necessary information
treatment_info = Treatments.WHERE(
    (start_dt >= start_of_period) & (start_dt < end_of_period)
).CALCULATE(
    treatment_year=YEAR(start_dt),
    treatment_month_num=MONTH(start_dt),
    patient_id=patient_id,
    is_biologic=(drug.drug_type == 'biologic')
)

# Partition by month and calculate the distinct patient counts
monthly_patient_counts = treatment_info.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_treatments",
    by=(treatment_year, treatment_month_num)
).CALCULATE(
    month=JOIN_STRINGS('-', treatment_year, LPAD(treatment_month_num, 2, '0')),
    patient_count=NDISTINCT(monthly_treatments.patient_id),

# Get treatments from the last 3 months excluding current month
treatments_last_3_months = Treatments.CALCULATE(
    treatment_month = JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(start_dt), LPAD(MONTH(start_dt), 2, "0")),
    patient_id = patient_id,
    is_biologic = drug.drug_type == "biologic"
).WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("months", start_dt, DATETIME("now")) >= 1 &
    DATEDIFF("months", start_dt, DATETIME("now")) <= 3
)

# Partition by month and calculate counts
result = treatments_last_3_months.PARTITION(
    name="months", 
    by=treatment_month
).CALCULATE(
    month = treatment_month,
    patient_count = NDISTINCT(Treatments.patient_id),
    biologic_treatment_count = NDISTINCT(Treatments.WHERE(is_biologic == 1).patient_id)
).ORDER_BY(month.DESC())

# Define the time window for the last three full months
time_window = DermTreatment.CALCULATE(
    start_of_current_month=DATETIME('now', 'start of month'),
    start_of_3_months_ago=DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-3 months')
)

# Filter treatments to this window and extract necessary information
treatment_info = time_window.Treatments.WHERE(
    (start_dt >= start_of_3_months_ago) & (start_dt < start_of_current_month)
).CALCULATE(
    treatment_month=JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(start_dt), LPAD(MONTH(start_dt), 2, "0")),
    patient_id=patient_id,
    is_biologic=(drug.drug_type == 'biologic')
)

# Group by month and calculate the distinct patient counts
monthly_patient_counts = treatment_info.PARTITION(name="monthly_groups", by=treatment_month).CALCULATE(
    month=treatment_month,
    patient_count=NDISTINCT(Treatments.patient_id),
    biologic_treatment_count=NDISTINCT(KEEP_IF(Treatments.patient_id, Treatments.is_biologic))
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# Get treatments from the last 12 months, excluding current month
treatments_last_12_months = Treatments.WHERE(
    (DATEDIFF("months", start_dt, DATETIME("now", "start of month")) <= 12) &
    (DATEDIFF("months", start_dt, DATETIME("now", "start of month")) > 0)
).CALCULATE(
    treatment_month = DATETIME(start_dt, "start of month"),
    patient_id = patient_id,
    diag_id = diag_id
)

# Partition by month and calculate PMPD and PMTC
result = treatments_last_12_months.PARTITION(
    name="months", 
    by=treatment_month
).CALCULATE(
    month = treatment_month,
    PMPD = NDISTINCT(Treatments.CALCULATE(patient_diag = JOIN_STRINGS("-", patient_id, diag_id)).patient_diag),
    PMTC = COUNT(Treatments)
).ORDER_BY(month.DESC())

# Define the start of the current month and the start of the 12-month period prior to it.
start_of_current_month = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
start_of_period = DATETIME(start_of_current_month, '-12 months')

# Filter treatments to include only those within the last 12 full months.
# Create a 'treatment_month' field by truncating the start_dt to the beginning of the month.
treatments_in_range = Treatments.WHERE(
    (start_dt >= start_of_period) & (start_dt < start_of_current_month)
).CALCULATE(
    treatment_month=DATETIME(start_dt, 'start of month')
)

# Partition the filtered treatments by month.
# For each month, calculate PMPD and PMTC.
# PMPD is the count of distinct patient-diagnosis pairs.
# PMTC is the total count of treatments.
monthly_kpis = treatments_in_range.PARTITION(name="monthly_groups", by=treatment_month).CALCULATE(
    month=treatment_month,
    PMPD=NDISTINCT(JOIN_STRINGS('-', monthly_groups.patient_id, monthly_groups.diag_id)),
    PMTC=COUNT(monthly_groups)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

[DEBUG] [Q71] Code generated by gemini:
# Define the start of the current month to establish the upper bound of our date range.
start_of_current_month = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')

# Define the start of our 12-month period, which is 12 months prior to the start of the current month.
start_of_period = DATETIME(start_of_current_month, '-12 months')

# Filter the Treatments collection to include only records within the last 12 full months.
treatments_in_range = Treatments.WHERE(
    (start_dt >= start_of_period) & (start_dt < start_of_current_month)
)

# For each filtered treatment, calculate the start of the month to use for aggregation.
treatments_with_month = treatments_in_range.CALCULATE(
    treatment_month=DATETIME(start_dt, 'start of month')
)

# Partition the data by the calculated month, then for each month, calculate PMPD and PMTC.
# PMPD (per month patient diagnoses) and PMTC (per month treatment count) are both derived
# by counting the number of treatments within each monthly group.
result = treatments_with_month.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_groups", by=(treatment_month)
).CALCULATE(
    month=treatment_month,
    PMPD=COUNT(Treatments),
    PMTC=COUNT(Treatments)
).ORDER_BY(treatment_month.ASC())

start_of_current_month = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
start_of_period = DATETIME(start_of_current_month, '-12 months')

# Filter treatments to the specified date range and calculate the month for aggregation.
treatments_in_range = Treatments.WHERE(
    (start_dt >= start_of_period) & (start_dt < start_of_current_month)
).CALCULATE(
    treatment_month=DATETIME(start_dt, 'start of month')
)

[DEBUG] [Q74] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: For each patient, identify their first treatment by ranking their treatments by start date.
# Then, extract the year of that first treatment.
first_treatment_info = Patients.treatments_received.WHERE(
    RANKING(by=start_dt.ASC(), per="Patients") == 1
).CALCULATE(
    first_treatment_year=YEAR(start_dt)
)

# Step 2: Group the results from Step 1 by the first treatment year.
# For each year, count the number of new patients.
yearly_new_patient_counts = first_treatment_info.PARTITION(
    name="new_patients_by_year", by=first_treatment_year
).CALCULATE(
    year=first_treatment_year,
    num_new_patients=COUNT(Patients)
)

# Step 3: Order the yearly counts and calculate the year-over-year increase (NPINPI).
# The PREV function retrieves the number of new patients from the previous year.
# A default value of 0 is used for the first year in the dataset.
npi_result = yearly_new_patient_counts.ORDER_BY(year.ASC()).CALCULATE(
    year=year,
    num_new_patients=num_new_patients,
    NPINPI=num_new_patients - PREV(num_new_patients, by=year.ASC(), default=0)
)

# Step 1: For each patient, find the year of their first treatment. This is the year they are considered a "new patient".
# We filter for patients who have received at least one treatment to ensure the MIN function has data to operate on.
new_patient_info = Patients.WHERE(HAS(treatments_received) == 1).CALCULATE(
    first_treatment_year=YEAR(MIN(treatments_received.start_dt))
)

# Step 2: Group the new patients by their first treatment year and count the number of patients in each year.
yearly_new_patients = new_patient_info.PARTITION(name="patients_by_year", by=first_treatment_year).CALCULATE(
    year=first_treatment_year,
    number_of_new_patients=COUNT(Patients)
)

# Step 3: Order the yearly counts chronologically and calculate the NPI (New Patients Increase).
# NPI is the difference between the number of new patients in the current year and the previous year.
# The PREV function retrieves the value from the preceding row after ordering.
result = yearly_new_patients.ORDER_BY(year.ASC()).CALCULATE(
    year=year,
    number_of_new_patients=number_of_new_patients,
    NPI=number_of_new_patients - PREV(number_of_new_patients, by=year.ASC())
)

# First, we filter the 'Outcomes' collection to include only records where both 
# 'day7_pasi_score' and 'day100_pasi_score' are not null.
# We use DEFAULT_TO to handle potential null values, ensuring that the filter is reliable.
valid_outcomes = Outcomes.WHERE(
    (DEFAULT_TO(day7_pasi_score, -1) >= 0) & (DEFAULT_TO(day100_pasi_score, -1) >= 0)
)

# Next, we calculate the average scores for day 7 and day 100 from the filtered data.
# Then, we apply the D7D100PIR formula to get the final percentage.
result = DermTreatment.CALCULATE(
    avg_day7_score=AVG(valid_outcomes.day7_pasi_score),
    avg_day100_score=AVG(valid_outcomes.day100_pasi_score)
).CALCULATE(
    d7d100pir=(avg_day100_score - avg_day7_score) / avg_day7_score * 100.0
)

# For each drug, count adverse events that occurred in the same month as treatment start
drug_adverse_counts = Drugs.CALCULATE(
    drug_id=drug_id,
    drug_name=drug_name,
    num_adverse_events=SUM(
        treatments_used_in.adverse_events.WHERE(
            (YEAR(reported_dt) == YEAR(treatment.start_dt)) & 
            (MONTH(reported_dt) == MONTH(treatment.start_dt))
        ).CALCULATE(count=1).count
    )
)

# Find the drug with the highest number of such adverse events
result = drug_adverse_counts.TOP_K(1, by=num_adverse_events.DESC())

# First, filter treatments to include only those that ended in 2022 and have associated outcome records.
# We also retrieve the insurance type from the related patient to use as a key for partitioning.
treatments_filtered = Treatments.WHERE(
    (YEAR(end_dt) == 2022) & (HAS(outcome_records) == 1)
).CALCULATE(
    ins_type_key=patient.ins_type
)

# Next, we partition the filtered treatments by the insurance type.
# For each insurance type group, we calculate the average day 100 PASI score and the number of distinct patients.
# Finally, we select the top 5 insurance types with the lowest average PASI score.
result = treatments_filtered.PARTITION(
    name="insurance_groups", by=(ins_type_key)
).CALCULATE(
    insurance_type=ins_type_key,
    avg_pasi_score=AVG(Treatments.outcome_records.day100_pasi_score),
    num_distinct_patients=NDISTINCT(Treatments.patient_id)
).TOP_K(5, by=avg_pasi_score.ASC())

    insurance_type = insurance_type,
    avg_pasi_score_day100 = AVG(Treatments.outcome_records.day100_pasi_score),
    num_distinct_patients = NDISTINCT(Treatments.patient_id)
).TOP_K(5, by=avg_pasi_score_day100.ASC())

# First, filter treatments to include only those that ended in 2022 and have associated outcome records.
# We also retrieve the insurance type from the related patient to use as a key for partitioning.
treatments_filtered = Treatments.WHERE(
    (YEAR(end_dt) == 2022) & (HAS(outcome_records) == 1)
).CALCULATE(
    ins_type_key=patient.ins_type
)

# Next, we partition the filtered treatments by the insurance type.
# For each insurance type group, we calculate the average day 100 PASI score and the number of distinct patients.
# Finally, we select the top 5 insurance types with the lowest average PASI score.
result = treatments_filtered.PARTITION(
    name="insurance_groups", by=(ins_type_key)
).CALCULATE(
    insurance_type=ins_type_key,
    avg_pasi_score=AVG(Treatments.outcome_records.day100_pasi_score),
    num_distinct_patients=NDISTINCT(Treatments.patient_id)
).TOP_K(5, by=avg_pasi_score.ASC())

[DEBUG] [Q86] Code generated by claude:
# Filter treatments started in the past 6 months and get doctor specialty
recent_treatments = Treatments.WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("days", start_dt, DATETIME("now")) <= 180
).CALCULATE(
    doctor_specialty=doctor.speciality,
    drug_amount=tot_drug_amt
)

# Group by specialty and calculate totals
result = recent_treatments.PARTITION(
    name="specialties", 
    by=doctor_specialty
).CALCULATE(
    specialty=doctor_specialty,
    num_treatments=COUNT(Treatments),
    total_drug_amount=SUM(Treatments.tot_drug_amt)
).TOP_K(3, by=total_drug_amount.DESC())

[DEBUG] [Q86] Code generated by gemini:
# Filter treatments started in the last 6 months and retrieve the doctor's specialty
treatments_in_period = Treatments.WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("months", start_dt, DATETIME('now')) < 6
).CALCULATE(
    doc_speciality=doctor.speciality
)

# Partition by specialty, calculate aggregates, and select the top 3
top_specialties_by_drug_amount = treatments_in_period.PARTITION(
    name="specialty_group", by=(doc_speciality)
).CALCULATE(
    specialty=doc_speciality,
    number_of_treatments=COUNT(Treatments),
    total_drug_amount=SUM(Treatments.tot_drug_amt)
).TOP_K(3, by=total_drug_amount.DESC())

# Get the start of the current week and calculate the date range for the last 3 weeks
# We need notifications from 4 weeks ago up to (but not including) the current week
notifications_info = Users.WHERE(
    (country == "US") | (country == "Canada")
).notifications.WHERE(
    # Filter for notifications in the last 3 weeks excluding current week
    (DATEDIFF("weeks", created_at, DATETIME("now")) >= 1) & 
    (DATEDIFF("weeks", created_at, DATETIME("now")) <= 3)
).CALCULATE(
    week_start = DATETIME(created_at, "start of week"),
    is_weekend = ISIN(DAYNAME(created_at), ("Saturday", "Sunday"))
)

# Group by week and calculate totals
result = notifications_info.PARTITION(
    name="weeks", 
    by=week_start
).CALCULATE(
    week_start = week_start,
    total_notifications = COUNT(Notifications),
    weekend_notifications = SUM(Notifications.is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week_start.DESC())

[DEBUG] [Q95] Code generated by gemini:
# First, filter notifications for users in the US and Canada that were created in the last 3 weeks, excluding the current week.
# For each of these notifications, we calculate the start of the week it belongs to and whether it occurred on a weekend.
notification_info = Users.WHERE(
    ISIN(country, ('US', 'Canada'))
).notifications.WHERE(
    (created_at >= DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-3 weeks')) & (created_at < DATETIME('now', 'start of week'))
).CALCULATE(
    notification_week=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend=ISIN(DAYNAME(created_at), ('Saturday', 'Sunday'))
)

# Then, we partition these notifications by the calculated week.
# For each week, we count the total number of notifications and sum the 'is_weekend' flag to get the count of weekend notifications.
result = notification_info.PARTITION(name="weekly_group", by=(notification_week)).CALCULATE(
    week=notification_week,
    total_notifications=COUNT(Notifications),
    weekend_notifications=SUM(is_weekend)
)

[DEBUG] [Q95] Code generated by gemini:
# Define the start of the current week to use as a reference for filtering
start_of_current_week = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')

# 1. Filter users from the US and Canada.
# 2. Access their notifications.
# 3. Filter notifications to include only those from the last 3 weeks, excluding the current week.
# 4. Calculate the week of the notification and a flag to identify if it was on a weekend.
notification_info = Users.WHERE(
    ISIN(country, ('US', 'Canada'))
).notifications.WHERE(
    (created_at >= DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-3 weeks')) & (created_at < start_of_current_week)
).CALCULATE(
    week=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend=IFF(ISIN(DAYNAME(created_at), ('Saturday', 'Sunday')), 1, 0)
)

# 5. Partition the prepared notification data by week.
# 6. For each week, calculate the total number of notifications and the sum of weekend notifications.
result = notification_info.PARTITION(name="weekly_group", by=(week)).CALCULATE(
    week=week,
    total_notifications=COUNT(Notifications),
    weekend_notifications=SUM(is_weekend)
)

# Filter user balances updated in the past 7 days
recent_balances = UserBalances.WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("days", updated_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 7
)

# Calculate average balance per user
user_avg_balances = recent_balances.PARTITION(name="users", by=user_id).CALCULATE(
    user_id=user_id,
    avg_balance=AVG(UserBalances.balance)
)

# Calculate the overall average AMB
result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    average_amb=AVG(user_avg_balances.avg_balance)

[DEBUG] [Q98] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Filter user balances to include only those updated in the last 7 days.
recent_user_balances = UserBalances.WHERE(DATEDIFF("days", updated_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 7)

# Step 2: Group the filtered balances by user_id and calculate the average balance for each user.
user_ambs = recent_user_balances.PARTITION(name="user_balances_group", by=(user_id)).CALCULATE(
    user_avg_balance=AVG(UserBalances.balance)
)

# Step 3: Calculate the final average of all user average balances.
average_balance_result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    average_amb=AVG(user_ambs.user_avg_balance)
)

[DEBUG] [Q102] Code generated by claude:
# Get transactions with sender_type = 0 from the last 2 months excluding current month
# First, filter for the date range and sender_type
wallet_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (sender_type == 0) & 
    (DATEDIFF("months", created_at, DATETIME("now")) >= 1) &
    (DATEDIFF("months", created_at, DATETIME("now")) <= 2)
).CALCULATE(
    sender_id=sender_id,
    month=DATETIME(created_at, "start of month")
)

# Partition by month and count distinct senders
result = wallet_transactions.PARTITION(
    name="months", 
    by=month
).CALCULATE(
    month=month,
    pmdau=NDISTINCT(Transactions.sender_id)
).ORDER_BY(month.DESC())

[DEBUG] [Q102] Code generated by gemini:
# Define the time boundaries for the last two months, excluding the current month.
time_boundaries = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    start_of_current_month=DATETIME('now', 'start of month'),
    start_of_filter_period=DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-2 months')
)

# Filter transactions to include only those from sender_type=0 within the specified time frame.
# Also, create a 'transaction_month' column by truncating the 'created_at' timestamp to the start of the month.
relevant_transactions = time_boundaries.Transactions.WHERE(
    (created_at >= start_of_filter_period) &
    (created_at < start_of_current_month) &
    (sender_type == 0)
).CALCULATE(
    transaction_month=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month')
)

# Partition the filtered transactions by month and calculate the PMDAU.
# PMDAU is the number of distinct senders for each month.
pmdau_analysis = relevant_transactions.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_groups", by=(transaction_month)
).CALCULATE(
    month=transaction_month,
    pmdau=NDISTINCT(Transactions.sender_id)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# Define the time boundaries for the last two full months
start_of_current_month = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
start_of_period = DATETIME(start_of_current_month, '-2 months')

# Calculate PMDAU for the specified period
result = Transactions.WHERE(

    (created_at < start_of_current_month) &

).CALCULATE(

).PARTITION(
    name="monthly_transactions", by=(transaction_month)
).CALCULATE(

[DEBUG] [Q107] Running models for: What was the STR for wallet transactions in the previous month?STR (success transaction rate) = number of successful transactions / total number of transactions. To determine if a transaction is successfull it's status has to be 'success'
[DEBUG] [Q107] Running model claude (attempt 0)
[DEBUG] [Q107] Running model gemini (attempt 0)
INFO:google_genai.models:AFC is enabled with max remote calls: 10.
INFO:httpx:HTTP Request: POST https://us-east5-aiplatform.googleapis.com/v1/projects/solid-drive-448717-p8/locations/us-east5/publishers/anthropic/models/claude-opus-4@20250514:streamRawPredict "HTTP/1.1 200 OK"
[DEBUG] [Q107] Code generated by claude:
# Get the start of the previous month
prev_month_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-1 day', 'start of month')

# Get the start of the current month (which is the end boundary for previous month)
current_month_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')

# Filter transactions from the previous month
prev_month_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (created_at >= prev_month_start) & 
    (created_at < current_month_start)
)

# Calculate STR
result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    str_percentage = 100.0 * COUNT(prev_month_transactions.WHERE(status == 'success')) / COUNT(prev_month_transactions),
    successful_transactions = COUNT(prev_month_transactions.WHERE(status == 'success')),
    total_transactions = COUNT(prev_month_transactions)
)
[DEBUG] [Q107] DataFrame from claude is valid
INFO:httpx:HTTP Request: POST https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-2.5-pro-preview-06-05:generateContent "HTTP/1.1 200 OK"
INFO:google_genai.models:AFC remote call 1 is done.
[DEBUG] [Q107] Code generated by gemini:
# Calculate the start of the current and previous months to define the time window
time_window = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    start_of_current_month=DATETIME('now', 'start of month'),
    start_of_previous_month=DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-1 month')
)

# Calculate the total and successful transactions within the previous month
transaction_counts = time_window.CALCULATE(
    total_transactions=COUNT(
        Transactions.WHERE(
            (created_at >= start_of_previous_month) & (created_at < start_of_current_month)
        )
    ),
    successful_transactions=COUNT(
        Transactions.WHERE(
            (created_at >= start_of_previous_month) & 
            (created_at < start_of_current_month) & 
            (status == 'success')
        )
    )
)

# Calculate the Success Transaction Rate (STR)
str_calculation = transaction_counts.CALCULATE(
    previous_month_str=IFF(
        total_transactions > 0,
        100.0 * successful_transactions / total_transactions,
        0.0
    )
)

result = str_calculation

        total_transactions > 0,

        0.0
    )
)

    (created_at < current_month_start)
)

    successful_transactions = COUNT(prev_month_transactions.WHERE(status == 'success')),

# Define the time window for the previous month using the DATETIME function.
# The start of the previous month is calculated by taking the start of the current month and subtracting one month.
start_of_previous_month = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-1 month')
start_of_current_month = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')

# Filter the 'Transactions' collection to include only those created in the previous month.
transactions_in_previous_month = Transactions.WHERE(
    (created_at >= start_of_previous_month) & (created_at < start_of_current_month)
)

# Calculate the number of successful and total transactions from the filtered set.
# A transaction is successful if its status is 'success'.
transaction_counts = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    successful_tx_count=COUNT(transactions_in_previous_month.WHERE(status == 'success')),
    total_tx_count=COUNT(transactions_in_previous_month)
)

# Calculate the Success Transaction Rate (STR) as a percentage.
# The IFF function is used to prevent division by zero if there were no transactions.
result = transaction_counts.CALCULATE(
    previous_month_str=IFF(total_tx_count > 0, 100.0 * successful_tx_count / total_tx_count, 0.0)
)

active_user_transactions_2023 = Users.WHERE(
    status == "active"
).transactions_sent.WHERE(
    (YEAR(created_at) == 2023) & 
    (status == "success") &
    (sender_type == 1)  # Assuming 1 represents user type
).CALCULATE(
    user_id=sender_id,
    year=YEAR(created_at),
    month=MONTH(created_at)
)

# Group by year and month, count distinct users
monthly_active_users = active_user_transactions_2023.PARTITION(
    name="months", 
    by=(year, month)
).CALCULATE(
    month_date=DATETIME(JOIN_STRINGS("-", year, LPAD(month, 2, "0"), "01")),
    num_active_users=NDISTINCT(months.user_id)
).ORDER_BY(month_date.ASC())

# First, filter transactions to include only those made in 2023 by active users that were successfully completed.
# We also create a 'transaction_month' field to represent the first day of the month for grouping.
transactions_info = Transactions.WHERE(
    (YEAR(created_at) == 2023) &
    (status == 'completed') &
    (HAS(sending_user) == 1) &
    (sending_user.status == 'active')
).CALCULATE(
    transaction_month=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month')
)

# Partition the filtered transactions by month and calculate the number of distinct sending users for each month.
# The result is then ordered by month.
active_users_per_month = transactions_info.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_transactions",
    by=(transaction_month)
).CALCULATE(
    month=transaction_month,
    distinct_active_users=NDISTINCT(Transactions.sender_id)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# First, define a contextless expression to filter for transactions that were
# received by a merchant within the last 150 days.
recent_merchant_transactions = transactions_received.WHERE(
    (receiver_type == 1) & (DATEDIFF("days", created_at, 'now') <= 150)
)

# Next, start with the Merchants collection and filter for those that have at least one
# relevant transaction. Then, calculate the total number of transactions and the total
# transaction amount for each merchant. Finally, select the top 2 merchants based on
# the total transaction amount in descending order.
top_merchants_by_amount = Merchants.WHERE(HAS(recent_merchant_transactions) == 1).CALCULATE(
    merchant_name=name,
    total_num_transactions=COUNT(recent_merchant_transactions),
    total_transaction_amount=SUM(recent_merchant_transactions.amount)
).TOP_K(2, by=total_transaction_amount.DESC())

# First, find the earliest snapshot date in 2023
earliest_date_info = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    earliest_snapshot_date=MIN(
        UserSettingSnapshots.WHERE(YEAR(snapshot_date) == 2023).snapshot_date
    )
)

# Then calculate the averages for snapshots on that earliest date
result = earliest_date_info.UserSettingSnapshots.WHERE(
    snapshot_date == earliest_snapshot_date
).CALCULATE(
    avg_daily_limit=AVG(UserSettingSnapshots.tx_limit_daily),
    avg_monthly_limit=AVG(UserSettingSnapshots.tx_limit_monthly)
)

# Step 1: For each merchant, find their single earliest coupon by start date.
# This is done by navigating to the coupons sub-collection and using the RANKING
# window function. The 'per="Merchants"' argument ensures that coupons are ranked
# independently for each merchant. We select the coupon with rank 1.
earliest_coupons = Merchants.coupons.WHERE(
    RANKING(by=start_date.ASC(), per="Merchants") == 1
)

# Step 2: Filter the results from Step 1.
# We keep only the merchants whose earliest coupon's start date is within 365 days
# of the merchant's registration date ('created_at'). The DATEDIFF function
# calculates the difference in days.
merchants_with_early_coupons = earliest_coupons.WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("days", merchant.created_at, start_date) <= 365
)

# Step 3: Select and format the final output fields.
# We retrieve the merchant's ID and registration date from the parent merchant record
# and the coupon's ID and start date from the coupon record itself.
result = merchants_with_early_coupons.CALCULATE(
    merchant_id=merchant.mid,
    registration_date=merchant.created_at,
    earliest_coupon_id=cid,
    earliest_coupon_start_date=start_date
)

[DEBUG] [Q123] Code generated by gemini:
# This contextless expression identifies the single earliest coupon for each merchant.
# RANKING is used with per="Merchants" to rank coupons by their start_date in ascending order for each merchant individually.
# We filter for rank 1 and use SINGULAR() because we need to access the properties of this single earliest coupon.
earliest_coupon = Merchants.coupons.WHERE(
    RANKING(by=start_date.ASC(), per="Merchants", allow_ties=True) == 1
).SINGULAR()

# The main query starts by filtering merchants to find those who meet the specified criteria.
# 1. HAS(coupons) == 1: Ensures we only consider merchants that have at least one coupon.
# 2. DATEDIFF(...) < 1: Checks if the difference in years between the merchant's registration date (created_at)
#    and their earliest coupon's start date is less than 1.
# Finally, CALCULATE selects the required information for the output.
result = Merchants.WHERE(
    (HAS(coupons) == 1) &
    (DATEDIFF("years", created_at, earliest_coupon.start_date) < 1)
).CALCULATE(
    merchant_id=mid,
    registration_date=created_at,
    earliest_coupon_id=earliest_coupon.cid,
    earliest_coupon_start_date=earliest_coupon.start_date
)
